### axioms.ttl (OWL axioms and SHACL rules over the vocabulary)
```turtle
@prefix lad:  <https://example.org/ladder/> .
@prefix owl:  <http://www.w3.org/2002/07/owl#> .
@prefix rdf:  <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#> .
@prefix rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#> .
@prefix sh:   <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix skos: <http://www.w3.org/2004/02/skos/core#> .
@prefix xsd:  <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

<https://example.org/ladder/axioms> a owl:Ontology ;
    rdfs:label "Credit ratings: axioms and rules" ;
    owl:imports <https://example.org/ladder/ontology> ;
    sh:declare [ sh:prefix "lad" ; sh:namespace "https://example.org/ladder/"^^xsd:anyURI ] ,
               [ sh:prefix "rdf" ; sh:namespace "http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#"^^xsd:anyURI ] .

# === Classes and properties =================================================================

lad:Entity a owl:Class ; rdfs:label "Entity" ; owl:hasKey ( lad:lei ) .
lad:Instrument a owl:Class ; rdfs:label "Instrument" ; owl:disjointWith lad:Entity .
lad:RatingAction a owl:Class ; rdfs:label "Rating action" .

lad:name a owl:DatatypeProperty ; rdfs:domain lad:Entity ; rdfs:range xsd:string .
lad:lei a owl:DatatypeProperty, owl:FunctionalProperty ; rdfs:domain lad:Entity ; rdfs:range xsd:string .
lad:country a owl:DatatypeProperty ; rdfs:domain lad:Entity ; rdfs:range xsd:string .
lad:entityType a owl:ObjectProperty, owl:FunctionalProperty ; rdfs:domain lad:Entity ; rdfs:range skos:Concept .
lad:sourceSystem a owl:DatatypeProperty, owl:FunctionalProperty ; rdfs:domain lad:Entity ; rdfs:range xsd:string .
lad:parent a owl:ObjectProperty ; rdfs:domain lad:Entity ; rdfs:range lad:Entity ;
    rdfs:comment "as this record gives it; see lad:parentEntity" .
lad:ancestor a owl:ObjectProperty, owl:TransitiveProperty ; rdfs:label "ancestor" .

lad:description a owl:DatatypeProperty ; rdfs:domain lad:Instrument .
lad:issuer a owl:ObjectProperty, owl:FunctionalProperty ; rdfs:domain lad:Instrument ; rdfs:range lad:Entity .
lad:guarantor a owl:ObjectProperty ; rdfs:domain lad:Instrument ; rdfs:range lad:Entity .
lad:seniority a owl:ObjectProperty, owl:FunctionalProperty ; rdfs:domain lad:Instrument ; rdfs:range skos:Concept .
lad:currency a owl:DatatypeProperty, owl:FunctionalProperty ; rdfs:domain lad:Instrument ; rdfs:range xsd:string .
lad:maturity a owl:DatatypeProperty ; rdfs:domain lad:Instrument ; rdfs:range xsd:date .

lad:date a owl:DatatypeProperty, owl:FunctionalProperty ; rdfs:domain lad:RatingAction ; rdfs:range xsd:date .
lad:ratedThing a owl:ObjectProperty, owl:FunctionalProperty ; rdfs:domain lad:RatingAction ;
    rdfs:range [ owl:unionOf ( lad:Entity lad:Instrument ) ] .
lad:ratingType a owl:ObjectProperty, owl:FunctionalProperty ; rdfs:domain lad:RatingAction ; rdfs:range skos:Concept .
lad:ccyBasis a owl:ObjectProperty, owl:FunctionalProperty ; rdfs:domain lad:RatingAction ; rdfs:range skos:Concept .
lad:action a owl:ObjectProperty, owl:FunctionalProperty ; rdfs:domain lad:RatingAction ; rdfs:range skos:Concept .
lad:rating a owl:DatatypeProperty, owl:FunctionalProperty ; rdfs:domain lad:RatingAction ; rdfs:range xsd:string .
lad:outlook a owl:ObjectProperty ; rdfs:domain lad:RatingAction ; rdfs:range skos:Concept .
lad:watch a owl:ObjectProperty ; rdfs:domain lad:RatingAction ; rdfs:range skos:Concept .
lad:comment a owl:DatatypeProperty ; rdfs:domain lad:RatingAction .

# Derived by the rules below.
lad:sameEntityAs a owl:ObjectProperty, owl:SymmetricProperty, owl:TransitiveProperty ; rdfs:subPropertyOf owl:sameAs .
lad:validTo a owl:DatatypeProperty ; rdfs:domain lad:RatingAction ; rdfs:range xsd:date .
lad:domicile a owl:DatatypeProperty, owl:FunctionalProperty ; rdfs:domain lad:Entity .
lad:parentEntity a owl:ObjectProperty ; rdfs:subPropertyOf lad:ancestor ; rdfs:label "parent, as the entity's RATINGS record gives it" .
lad:ultimateParent a owl:ObjectProperty ; rdfs:label "ultimate parent: the ancestor with no parent, or the entity itself" .
lad:creditRiskOf a owl:ObjectProperty, owl:FunctionalProperty ; rdfs:domain lad:Instrument ; rdfs:range lad:Entity .
lad:localCurrency a owl:DatatypeProperty ; rdfs:domain lad:Instrument ; rdfs:range xsd:boolean .

# === Code lists as classes of actions ======================================================

lad:IssuerRatingAction owl:equivalentClass [ a owl:Class ; owl:unionOf (
        [ a owl:Restriction ; owl:onProperty lad:ratingType ; owl:hasValue lad:type-LTIDR ]
        [ a owl:Restriction ; owl:onProperty lad:ratingType ; owl:hasValue lad:type-STIDR ] ) ] .
lad:InstrumentRatingAction owl:equivalentClass [ a owl:Restriction ; owl:onProperty lad:ratingType ; owl:hasValue lad:type-INST ] ;
    owl:disjointWith lad:IssuerRatingAction .
lad:CeilingAction owl:equivalentClass [ a owl:Restriction ; owl:onProperty lad:ratingType ; owl:hasValue lad:type-CC ] ;
    owl:disjointWith lad:IssuerRatingAction, lad:InstrumentRatingAction .
lad:CreditOpinionAction owl:equivalentClass [ a owl:Restriction ; owl:onProperty lad:ratingType ; owl:hasValue lad:type-CO ] ;
    owl:disjointWith lad:IssuerRatingAction, lad:InstrumentRatingAction, lad:RatingInForce .

lad:Downgrade owl:equivalentClass [ a owl:Restriction ; owl:onProperty lad:action ; owl:hasValue lad:action-DG ] .
lad:Upgrade owl:equivalentClass [ a owl:Restriction ; owl:onProperty lad:action ; owl:hasValue lad:action-UG ] .
lad:RatingChange owl:equivalentClass [ a owl:Class ; owl:unionOf ( lad:Downgrade lad:Upgrade ) ] .
lad:WatchPlacement owl:equivalentClass [ a owl:Restriction ; owl:onProperty lad:action ; owl:hasValue lad:action-RW ] ;
    owl:disjointWith lad:RatingChange .
lad:Affirmation owl:equivalentClass [ a owl:Restriction ; owl:onProperty lad:action ; owl:hasValue lad:action-AF ] ;
    owl:disjointWith lad:RatingChange .
lad:Withdrawal owl:equivalentClass [ a owl:Restriction ; owl:onProperty lad:action ; owl:hasValue lad:action-WD ] ;
    owl:disjointWith lad:RatingChange, lad:DefaultRating, lad:RatingInForce .
lad:ExpectedRating owl:equivalentClass [ a owl:Restriction ; owl:onProperty lad:action ; owl:hasValue lad:action-EX ] ;
    owl:disjointWith lad:RatingInForce .
lad:DefaultRating owl:equivalentClass [ a owl:Restriction ; owl:onProperty lad:rating ; owl:oneOf ( "RD" "D" ) ] .

lad:TestRecord owl:equivalentClass [ a owl:Restriction ; owl:onProperty lad:sourceSystem ; owl:hasValue "QA" ] ;
    owl:disjointWith lad:LegalEntity, lad:Issuer .
lad:LegalEntity rdfs:label "legal entity" ; owl:equivalentClass [ a owl:Class ; owl:intersectionOf (
        lad:Entity [ a owl:Class ; owl:complementOf lad:TestRecord ] ) ] .
lad:Issuer owl:equivalentClass [ a owl:Restriction ; owl:onProperty [ owl:inverseOf lad:ratedThing ] ;
        owl:someValuesFrom [ owl:intersectionOf ( lad:IssuerRatingAction lad:RatingInForce ) ] ] .

# === The rating scales ======================================================================

lad:LongTermScale rdfs:label "long-term scale, strongest first" ;
    rdf:value ( "AAA" "AA+" "AA" "AA-" "A+" "A" "A-" "BBB+" "BBB" "BBB-" "BB+" "BB" "BB-" "B+" "B" "B-" "CCC" "CC" "C" "RD" "D" ) .
lad:InvestmentGradeRatings rdf:value ( "AAA" "AA+" "AA" "AA-" "A+" "A" "A-" "BBB+" "BBB" "BBB-" ) .
lad:ShortTermScale rdfs:label "short-term scale, strongest first" ;
    rdf:value ( "F1+" "F1" "F2" "F3" "B" "C" "RD" "D" ) .

# === Currencies =============================================================================

lad:domesticCurrency a owl:DatatypeProperty ; rdfs:label "domestic currency (country code, currency code)" .
lad:VD lad:domesticCurrency "XVD" .   lad:CV lad:domesticCurrency "CVK" .
lad:GB lad:domesticCurrency "GBP" .   lad:US lad:domesticCurrency "USD" .
lad:DE lad:domesticCurrency "EUR" .   lad:FR lad:domesticCurrency "EUR" .   lad:NL lad:domesticCurrency "EUR" .
lad:GR lad:domesticCurrency "EUR" .   lad:IT lad:domesticCurrency "EUR" .   lad:ES lad:domesticCurrency "EUR" .
lad:LU lad:domesticCurrency "EUR" .

# === Rules (SHACL-AF), run in this order ====================================================

lad:IdentityRule a sh:NodeShape ; sh:targetClass lad:Entity ;
    sh:rule [ a sh:SPARQLRule ; sh:order 1 ; sh:prefixes <https://example.org/ladder/axioms> ; sh:construct """
        CONSTRUCT { $this lad:sameEntityAs ?other }
        WHERE { $this lad:lei ?lei . ?other lad:lei ?lei . FILTER (?other != $this) }""" ] ;
    sh:rule [ a sh:SPARQLRule ; sh:order 2 ; sh:prefixes <https://example.org/ladder/axioms> ; sh:construct """
        CONSTRUCT { $this lad:domicile ?c }
        WHERE {
          { $this lad:sourceSystem "RESEARCH" ; lad:country ?c }
          UNION
          { $this lad:sameEntityAs ?r . ?r lad:sourceSystem "RESEARCH" ; lad:country ?c }
          UNION
          { $this lad:country ?c FILTER NOT EXISTS { $this lad:sameEntityAs/lad:sourceSystem "RESEARCH" }
                                 FILTER NOT EXISTS { $this lad:sourceSystem "RESEARCH" } }
        }""" ] ;
    sh:rule [ a sh:SPARQLRule ; sh:order 2 ; sh:prefixes <https://example.org/ladder/axioms> ; sh:construct """
        CONSTRUCT { $this lad:parentEntity ?p , ?p2 }
        WHERE {
          { $this lad:sourceSystem "RATINGS" ; lad:parent ?p }
          UNION
          { $this lad:sameEntityAs ?r . ?r lad:sourceSystem "RATINGS" ; lad:parent ?p }
          UNION
          { $this lad:parent ?p FILTER NOT EXISTS { $this lad:sameEntityAs/lad:sourceSystem "RATINGS" }
                                FILTER NOT EXISTS { $this lad:sourceSystem "RATINGS" } }
          OPTIONAL { ?p lad:sameEntityAs ?p2 }
          FILTER NOT EXISTS { $this lad:sourceSystem "QA" }
        }""" ] ;
    sh:rule [ a sh:SPARQLRule ; sh:order 2.5 ; sh:prefixes <https://example.org/ladder/axioms> ; sh:construct """
        CONSTRUCT { $this lad:ancestor ?a }
        WHERE { $this lad:parentEntity+ ?a }""" ] ;
    sh:rule [ a sh:SPARQLRule ; sh:order 2.6 ; sh:prefixes <https://example.org/ladder/axioms> ; sh:construct """
        CONSTRUCT { $this lad:ultimateParent ?u }
        WHERE {
          FILTER NOT EXISTS { $this lad:sourceSystem "QA" }
          { $this lad:ancestor ?u FILTER NOT EXISTS { ?u lad:parentEntity ?x } }
          UNION
          { BIND ($this AS ?u) FILTER NOT EXISTS { $this lad:parentEntity ?x } }
        }""" ] .

lad:RatingPeriodRule a sh:NodeShape ; sh:targetClass lad:RatingAction ;
    sh:rule [ a sh:SPARQLRule ; sh:order 3 ; sh:prefixes <https://example.org/ladder/axioms> ; sh:construct """
        CONSTRUCT { $this lad:validTo ?to }
        WHERE {
          $this lad:ratedThing ?t ; lad:ratingType ?type ; lad:date ?d .
          OPTIONAL { $this lad:ccyBasis ?basis }
          { SELECT $this (MIN(?d2) AS ?to) WHERE {
              $this lad:ratedThing ?t ; lad:ratingType ?type ; lad:date ?d .
              OPTIONAL { $this lad:ccyBasis ?basis }
              ?later lad:ratingType ?type ; lad:date ?d2 ; lad:ratedThing ?t2 .
              OPTIONAL { ?later lad:ccyBasis ?basis2 }
              FILTER (?d2 > ?d && COALESCE(?basis2, "") = COALESCE(?basis, ""))
              FILTER (?t2 = ?t || EXISTS { ?t2 lad:sameEntityAs ?t })
              FILTER NOT EXISTS { ?later lad:action lad:action-EX }
            } GROUP BY $this }
        }""" ] ;
    sh:rule [ a sh:SPARQLRule ; sh:order 4 ; sh:prefixes <https://example.org/ladder/axioms> ; sh:construct """
        CONSTRUCT { $this a lad:RatingInForce }
        WHERE {
          $this lad:ratedThing ?t .
          FILTER NOT EXISTS { $this lad:validTo ?to }
          FILTER NOT EXISTS { $this lad:action lad:action-WD }
          FILTER NOT EXISTS { $this lad:action lad:action-EX }
          FILTER NOT EXISTS { $this lad:ratingType lad:type-CO }
          FILTER NOT EXISTS { ?t lad:sourceSystem "QA" }
        }""" ] .

lad:InstrumentRule a sh:NodeShape ; sh:targetClass lad:Instrument ;
    sh:rule [ a sh:SPARQLRule ; sh:order 5 ; sh:prefixes <https://example.org/ladder/axioms> ; sh:construct """
        CONSTRUCT { $this lad:creditRiskOf ?who ; lad:localCurrency ?local }
        WHERE {
          $this lad:issuer ?issuer ; lad:currency ?ccy .
          OPTIONAL { $this lad:guarantor ?g }
          BIND (COALESCE(?g, ?issuer) AS ?who)
          ?issuer lad:domicile ?c .
          BIND (IRI(CONCAT(STR(lad:), ?c)) AS ?country)
          OPTIONAL { ?country lad:domesticCurrency ?home }
          BIND (?ccy = COALESCE(?home, "") AS ?local)
        }""" ] .

# === Constraints (SHACL) ====================================================================

lad:CeilingShape a sh:NodeShape ; sh:targetClass lad:RatingInForce ;
    sh:sparql [ sh:prefixes <https://example.org/ladder/axioms> ; sh:message "a foreign-currency rating above its country's ceiling" ; sh:select """
        SELECT $this WHERE {
          $this lad:ccyBasis lad:basis-FC ; lad:ratingType lad:type-LTIDR ; lad:rating ?r ; lad:ratedThing ?e .
          ?e lad:domicile ?c ; lad:entityType ?type . FILTER (?type != lad:etype-SOV)
          ?sov lad:entityType lad:etype-SOV ; lad:country ?c .
          ?ceil lad:ratedThing ?sov ; lad:ratingType lad:type-CC ; lad:rating ?cap ; a lad:RatingInForce .
          lad:LongTermScale rdf:value ?scale .
          ?scale rdf:rest* ?fromR . ?fromR rdf:first ?r .
          ?scale rdf:rest* ?fromCap . ?fromCap rdf:first ?cap .
          FILTER NOT EXISTS { ?fromCap rdf:rest* ?fromR }
        }""" ] .

lad:TestRecordShape a sh:NodeShape ; sh:targetClass lad:TestRecord ;
    sh:property [ sh:path [ sh:inversePath lad:ratedThing ] ; sh:maxCount 0 ; sh:severity sh:Info ;
                  sh:message "a test record's actions are not rating actions" ] .
```

## The data

### entities.csv

### ontology.ttl, without its definitions (the vocabulary the data uses)
The codes in the CSV files are the skos:notation values of the concepts below.

### axioms.ttl (OWL axioms and SHACL rules over the vocabulary)
```turtle
@prefix lad:  <https://example.org/ladder/> .
@prefix owl:  <http://www.w3.org/2002/07/owl#> .
@prefix rdf:  <http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#> .
@prefix rdfs: <http://www.w3.org/2000/01/rdf-schema#> .
@prefix sh:   <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix skos: <http://www.w3.org/2004/02/skos/core#> .
@prefix xsd:  <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

<https://example.org/ladder/axioms> a owl:Ontology ;
    rdfs:label "Credit ratings: axioms and rules" ;
    owl:imports <https://example.org/ladder/ontology> ;
    sh:declare [ sh:prefix "lad" ; sh:namespace "https://example.org/ladder/"^^xsd:anyURI ] ,
               [ sh:prefix "rdf" ; sh:namespace "http://www.w3.org/1999/02/22-rdf-syntax-ns#"^^xsd:anyURI ] .

# === Classes and properties =================================================================

lad:Entity a owl:Class ; rdfs:label "Entity" ; owl:hasKey ( lad:lei ) .
lad:Instrument a owl:Class ; rdfs:label "Instrument" ; owl:disjointWith lad:Entity .
lad:RatingAction a owl:Class ; rdfs:label "Rating action" .

lad:name a owl:DatatypeProperty ; rdfs:domain lad:Entity ; rdfs:range xsd:string .
lad:lei a owl:DatatypeProperty, owl:FunctionalProperty ; rdfs:domain lad:Entity ; rdfs:range xsd:string .
lad:country a owl:DatatypeProperty ; rdfs:domain lad:Entity ; rdfs:range xsd:string .
lad:entityType a owl:ObjectProperty, owl:FunctionalProperty ; rdfs:domain lad:Entity ; rdfs:range skos:Concept .
lad:sourceSystem a owl:DatatypeProperty, owl:FunctionalProperty ; rdfs:domain lad:Entity ; rdfs:range xsd:string .
lad:parent a owl:ObjectProperty ; rdfs:domain lad:Entity ; rdfs:range lad:Entity ;
    rdfs:comment "as this record gives it; see lad:parentEntity" .
lad:ancestor a owl:ObjectProperty, owl:TransitiveProperty ; rdfs:label "ancestor" .

lad:description a owl:DatatypeProperty ; rdfs:domain lad:Instrument .
lad:issuer a owl:ObjectProperty, owl:FunctionalProperty ; rdfs:domain lad:Instrument ; rdfs:range lad:Entity .
lad:guarantor a owl:ObjectProperty ; rdfs:domain lad:Instrument ; rdfs:range lad:Entity .
lad:seniority a owl:ObjectProperty, owl:FunctionalProperty ; rdfs:domain lad:Instrument ; rdfs:range skos:Concept .
lad:currency a owl:DatatypeProperty, owl:FunctionalProperty ; rdfs:domain lad:Instrument ; rdfs:range xsd:string .
lad:maturity a owl:DatatypeProperty ; rdfs:domain lad:Instrument ; rdfs:range xsd:date .

lad:date a owl:DatatypeProperty, owl:FunctionalProperty ; rdfs:domain lad:RatingAction ; rdfs:range xsd:date .
lad:ratedThing a owl:ObjectProperty, owl:FunctionalProperty ; rdfs:domain lad:RatingAction ;
    rdfs:range [ owl:unionOf ( lad:Entity lad:Instrument ) ] .
lad:ratingType a owl:ObjectProperty, owl:FunctionalProperty ; rdfs:domain lad:RatingAction ; rdfs:range skos:Concept .
lad:ccyBasis a owl:ObjectProperty, owl:FunctionalProperty ; rdfs:domain lad:RatingAction ; rdfs:range skos:Concept .
lad:action a owl:ObjectProperty, owl:FunctionalProperty ; rdfs:domain lad:RatingAction ; rdfs:range skos:Concept .
lad:rating a owl:DatatypeProperty, owl:FunctionalProperty ; rdfs:domain lad:RatingAction ; rdfs:range xsd:string .
lad:outlook a owl:ObjectProperty ; rdfs:domain lad:RatingAction ; rdfs:range skos:Concept .
lad:watch a owl:ObjectProperty ; rdfs:domain lad:RatingAction ; rdfs:range skos:Concept .
lad:comment a owl:DatatypeProperty ; rdfs:domain lad:RatingAction .

# Derived by the rules below.
lad:sameEntityAs a owl:ObjectProperty, owl:SymmetricProperty, owl:TransitiveProperty ; rdfs:subPropertyOf owl:sameAs .
lad:validTo a owl:DatatypeProperty ; rdfs:domain lad:RatingAction ; rdfs:range xsd:date .
lad:domicile a owl:DatatypeProperty, owl:FunctionalProperty ; rdfs:domain lad:Entity .
lad:parentEntity a owl:ObjectProperty ; rdfs:subPropertyOf lad:ancestor ; rdfs:label "parent, as the entity's RATINGS record gives it" .
lad:ultimateParent a owl:ObjectProperty ; rdfs:label "ultimate parent: the ancestor with no parent, or the entity itself" .
lad:creditRiskOf a owl:ObjectProperty, owl:FunctionalProperty ; rdfs:domain lad:Instrument ; rdfs:range lad:Entity .
lad:localCurrency a owl:DatatypeProperty ; rdfs:domain lad:Instrument ; rdfs:range xsd:boolean .

# === Code lists as classes of actions ======================================================

lad:IssuerRatingAction owl:equivalentClass [ a owl:Class ; owl:unionOf (
        [ a owl:Restriction ; owl:onProperty lad:ratingType ; owl:hasValue lad:type-LTIDR ]
        [ a owl:Restriction ; owl:onProperty lad:ratingType ; owl:hasValue lad:type-STIDR ] ) ] .
lad:InstrumentRatingAction owl:equivalentClass [ a owl:Restriction ; owl:onProperty lad:ratingType ; owl:hasValue lad:type-INST ] ;
    owl:disjointWith lad:IssuerRatingAction .
lad:CeilingAction owl:equivalentClass [ a owl:Restriction ; owl:onProperty lad:ratingType ; owl:hasValue lad:type-CC ] ;
    owl:disjointWith lad:IssuerRatingAction, lad:InstrumentRatingAction .
lad:CreditOpinionAction owl:equivalentClass [ a owl:Restriction ; owl:onProperty lad:ratingType ; owl:hasValue lad:type-CO ] ;
    owl:disjointWith lad:IssuerRatingAction, lad:InstrumentRatingAction, lad:RatingInForce .

lad:Downgrade owl:equivalentClass [ a owl:Restriction ; owl:onProperty lad:action ; owl:hasValue lad:action-DG ] .
lad:Upgrade owl:equivalentClass [ a owl:Restriction ; owl:onProperty lad:action ; owl:hasValue lad:action-UG ] .
lad:RatingChange owl:equivalentClass [ a owl:Class ; owl:unionOf ( lad:Downgrade lad:Upgrade ) ] .
lad:WatchPlacement owl:equivalentClass [ a owl:Restriction ; owl:onProperty lad:action ; owl:hasValue lad:action-RW ] ;
    owl:disjointWith lad:RatingChange .
lad:Affirmation owl:equivalentClass [ a owl:Restriction ; owl:onProperty lad:action ; owl:hasValue lad:action-AF ] ;
    owl:disjointWith lad:RatingChange .
lad:Withdrawal owl:equivalentClass [ a owl:Restriction ; owl:onProperty lad:action ; owl:hasValue lad:action-WD ] ;
    owl:disjointWith lad:RatingChange, lad:DefaultRating, lad:RatingInForce .
lad:ExpectedRating owl:equivalentClass [ a owl:Restriction ; owl:onProperty lad:action ; owl:hasValue lad:action-EX ] ;
    owl:disjointWith lad:RatingInForce .
lad:DefaultRating owl:equivalentClass [ a owl:Restriction ; owl:onProperty lad:rating ; owl:oneOf ( "RD" "D" ) ] .

lad:TestRecord owl:equivalentClass [ a owl:Restriction ; owl:onProperty lad:sourceSystem ; owl:hasValue "QA" ] ;
    owl:disjointWith lad:LegalEntity, lad:Issuer .
lad:LegalEntity rdfs:label "legal entity" ; owl:equivalentClass [ a owl:Class ; owl:intersectionOf (
        lad:Entity [ a owl:Class ; owl:complementOf lad:TestRecord ] ) ] .
lad:Issuer owl:equivalentClass [ a owl:Restriction ; owl:onProperty [ owl:inverseOf lad:ratedThing ] ;
        owl:someValuesFrom [ owl:intersectionOf ( lad:IssuerRatingAction lad:RatingInForce ) ] ] .

# === The rating scales ======================================================================

lad:LongTermScale rdfs:label "long-term scale, strongest first" ;
    rdf:value ( "AAA" "AA+" "AA" "AA-" "A+" "A" "A-" "BBB+" "BBB" "BBB-" "BB+" "BB" "BB-" "B+" "B" "B-" "CCC" "CC" "C" "RD" "D" ) .
lad:InvestmentGradeRatings rdf:value ( "AAA" "AA+" "AA" "AA-" "A+" "A" "A-" "BBB+" "BBB" "BBB-" ) .
lad:ShortTermScale rdfs:label "short-term scale, strongest first" ;
    rdf:value ( "F1+" "F1" "F2" "F3" "B" "C" "RD" "D" ) .

# === Currencies =============================================================================

lad:domesticCurrency a owl:DatatypeProperty ; rdfs:label "domestic currency (country code, currency code)" .
lad:VD lad:domesticCurrency "XVD" .   lad:CV lad:domesticCurrency "CVK" .
lad:GB lad:domesticCurrency "GBP" .   lad:US lad:domesticCurrency "USD" .
lad:DE lad:domesticCurrency "EUR" .   lad:FR lad:domesticCurrency "EUR" .   lad:NL lad:domesticCurrency "EUR" .
lad:GR lad:domesticCurrency "EUR" .   lad:IT lad:domesticCurrency "EUR" .   lad:ES lad:domesticCurrency "EUR" .
lad:LU lad:domesticCurrency "EUR" .

# === Rules (SHACL-AF), run in this order ====================================================

lad:IdentityRule a sh:NodeShape ; sh:targetClass lad:Entity ;
    sh:rule [ a sh:SPARQLRule ; sh:order 1 ; sh:prefixes <https://example.org/ladder/axioms> ; sh:construct """
        CONSTRUCT { $this lad:sameEntityAs ?other }
        WHERE { $this lad:lei ?lei . ?other lad:lei ?lei . FILTER (?other != $this) }""" ] ;
    sh:rule [ a sh:SPARQLRule ; sh:order 2 ; sh:prefixes <https://example.org/ladder/axioms> ; sh:construct """
        CONSTRUCT { $this lad:domicile ?c }
        WHERE {
          { $this lad:sourceSystem "RESEARCH" ; lad:country ?c }
          UNION
          { $this lad:sameEntityAs ?r . ?r lad:sourceSystem "RESEARCH" ; lad:country ?c }
          UNION
          { $this lad:country ?c FILTER NOT EXISTS { $this lad:sameEntityAs/lad:sourceSystem "RESEARCH" }
                                 FILTER NOT EXISTS { $this lad:sourceSystem "RESEARCH" } }
        }""" ] ;
    sh:rule [ a sh:SPARQLRule ; sh:order 2 ; sh:prefixes <https://example.org/ladder/axioms> ; sh:construct """
        CONSTRUCT { $this lad:parentEntity ?p , ?p2 }
        WHERE {
          { $this lad:sourceSystem "RATINGS" ; lad:parent ?p }
          UNION
          { $this lad:sameEntityAs ?r . ?r lad:sourceSystem "RATINGS" ; lad:parent ?p }
          UNION
          { $this lad:parent ?p FILTER NOT EXISTS { $this lad:sameEntityAs/lad:sourceSystem "RATINGS" }
                                FILTER NOT EXISTS { $this lad:sourceSystem "RATINGS" } }
          OPTIONAL { ?p lad:sameEntityAs ?p2 }
          FILTER NOT EXISTS { $this lad:sourceSystem "QA" }
        }""" ] ;
    sh:rule [ a sh:SPARQLRule ; sh:order 2.5 ; sh:prefixes <https://example.org/ladder/axioms> ; sh:construct """
        CONSTRUCT { $this lad:ancestor ?a }
        WHERE { $this lad:parentEntity+ ?a }""" ] ;
    sh:rule [ a sh:SPARQLRule ; sh:order 2.6 ; sh:prefixes <https://example.org/ladder/axioms> ; sh:construct """
        CONSTRUCT { $this lad:ultimateParent ?u }
        WHERE {
          FILTER NOT EXISTS { $this lad:sourceSystem "QA" }
          { $this lad:ancestor ?u FILTER NOT EXISTS { ?u lad:parentEntity ?x } }
          UNION
          { BIND ($this AS ?u) FILTER NOT EXISTS { $this lad:parentEntity ?x } }
        }""" ] .

lad:RatingPeriodRule a sh:NodeShape ; sh:targetClass lad:RatingAction ;
    sh:rule [ a sh:SPARQLRule ; sh:order 3 ; sh:prefixes <https://example.org/ladder/axioms> ; sh:construct """
        CONSTRUCT { $this lad:validTo ?to }
        WHERE {
          $this lad:ratedThing ?t ; lad:ratingType ?type ; lad:date ?d .
          OPTIONAL { $this lad:ccyBasis ?basis }
          { SELECT $this (MIN(?d2) AS ?to) WHERE {
              $this lad:ratedThing ?t ; lad:ratingType ?type ; lad:date ?d .
              OPTIONAL { $this lad:ccyBasis ?basis }
              ?later lad:ratingType ?type ; lad:date ?d2 ; lad:ratedThing ?t2 .
              OPTIONAL { ?later lad:ccyBasis ?basis2 }
              FILTER (?d2 > ?d && COALESCE(?basis2, "") = COALESCE(?basis, ""))
              FILTER (?t2 = ?t || EXISTS { ?t2 lad:sameEntityAs ?t })
              FILTER NOT EXISTS { ?later lad:action lad:action-EX }
            } GROUP BY $this }
        }""" ] ;
    sh:rule [ a sh:SPARQLRule ; sh:order 4 ; sh:prefixes <https://example.org/ladder/axioms> ; sh:construct """
        CONSTRUCT { $this a lad:RatingInForce }
        WHERE {
          $this lad:ratedThing ?t .
          FILTER NOT EXISTS { $this lad:validTo ?to }
          FILTER NOT EXISTS { $this lad:action lad:action-WD }
          FILTER NOT EXISTS { $this lad:action lad:action-EX }
          FILTER NOT EXISTS { $this lad:ratingType lad:type-CO }
          FILTER NOT EXISTS { ?t lad:sourceSystem "QA" }
        }""" ] .

lad:InstrumentRule a sh:NodeShape ; sh:targetClass lad:Instrument ;
    sh:rule [ a sh:SPARQLRule ; sh:order 5 ; sh:prefixes <https://example.org/ladder/axioms> ; sh:construct """
        CONSTRUCT { $this lad:creditRiskOf ?who ; lad:localCurrency ?local }
        WHERE {
          $this lad:issuer ?issuer ; lad:currency ?ccy .
          OPTIONAL { $this lad:guarantor ?g }
          BIND (COALESCE(?g, ?issuer) AS ?who)
          ?issuer lad:domicile ?c .
          BIND (IRI(CONCAT(STR(lad:), ?c)) AS ?country)
          OPTIONAL { ?country lad:domesticCurrency ?home }
          BIND (?ccy = COALESCE(?home, "") AS ?local)
        }""" ] .

# === Constraints (SHACL) ====================================================================

lad:CeilingShape a sh:NodeShape ; sh:targetClass lad:RatingInForce ;
    sh:sparql [ sh:prefixes <https://example.org/ladder/axioms> ; sh:message "a foreign-currency rating above its country's ceiling" ; sh:select """
        SELECT $this WHERE {
          $this lad:ccyBasis lad:basis-FC ; lad:ratingType lad:type-LTIDR ; lad:rating ?r ; lad:ratedThing ?e .
          ?e lad:domicile ?c ; lad:entityType ?type . FILTER (?type != lad:etype-SOV)
          ?sov lad:entityType lad:etype-SOV ; lad:country ?c .
          ?ceil lad:ratedThing ?sov ; lad:ratingType lad:type-CC ; lad:rating ?cap ; a lad:RatingInForce .
          lad:LongTermScale rdf:value ?scale .
          ?scale rdf:rest* ?fromR . ?fromR rdf:first ?r .
          ?scale rdf:rest* ?fromCap . ?fromCap rdf:first ?cap .
          FILTER NOT EXISTS { ?fromCap rdf:rest* ?fromR }
        }""" ] .

lad:TestRecordShape a sh:NodeShape ; sh:targetClass lad:TestRecord ;
    sh:property [ sh:path [ sh:inversePath lad:ratedThing ] ; sh:maxCount 0 ; sh:severity sh:Info ;
                  sh:message "a test record's actions are not rating actions" ] .
```

### ontology.md (the vocabulary the data uses)

# Credit ratings: a teaching ontology

Entities, instruments, rating actions and the codes the data uses, each with a definition that states what it means and how to read it. Invented for an experiment; not a model of any real rating agency.

## Classes

### Entity

A legal entity: a company, bank, finance subsidiary or sovereign. An entity is identified by its LEI (LEI), not by its name or record ID. The data holds records from more than one source system (source system), so the same entity can appear as several records with different IDs and differently spelled names: two records with the same LEI are the same entity, and every fact recorded against either record is a fact about that one entity.

### Instrument

A debt instrument, such as a bond or note, issued by an entity (issuer). An instrument's rating is not the issuer's rating: it can sit above or below it depending on seniority (seniority) and on any guarantee (guarantor).

### Rating action

Something a rating agency did to a rating on a date (date): assigned, affirmed, upgraded, downgraded, placed on Watch or withdrawn it. Each action applies to one rated thing (rated thing), one rating type (rating type) and, for issuer ratings, one currency basis (currency basis). A rating holds from its action date until the next action on the same thing, type and basis: so the rating on a date D is the one set by the latest action on or before D, and the current rating is the latest action overall. rating always holds the rating as it stands after the action, including when it did not change.

## Properties

### name (CSV column `name`)

The name as the source system spells it. Names are not identifiers: the same entity can be spelled differently in different systems.

### LEI (CSV column `lei`)

Legal Entity Identifier: the identifier that says which legal entity a record describes. Same LEI, same entity. Sovereigns have none.

### country (CSV column `country`)

ISO-style code of the country where the entity is domiciled. Where an entity's records disagree, see source system for which holds. It decides which Country Ceiling, if any, applies to the entity's foreign-currency ratings. VD is the Republic of Valdoria and CV the Republic of Corvenia (both invented).

### entity type (CSV column `entity_type`)

What kind of entity it is: a concept of the scheme Entity types.

### parent (CSV column `parent_id`)

The entity that owns and controls this one. A parent link names a record, but points at the entity that record describes, so the chain continues from any of that entity's records. Following parents up to an entity with no parent gives the ultimate parent; a group is an ultimate parent and every entity whose chain of parents ends at it.

### source system (CSV column `source_system`)

The internal system the record comes from (RATINGS, RESEARCH, QA). Each system keeps its own records of the same entities; see Entity. Where records of the same entity disagree, which one holds depends on the field. RATINGS is the system of record for names and parent links. Domicile is the exception: the RESEARCH team checks each entity's registered office against its filings, so where records disagree on country, the RESEARCH record holds. QA records are test data, created to check the systems: they describe no real entity, so a QA record, and every action recorded against it, must be ignored.

### description (CSV column `description`)

The instrument's name: issuer, coupon and maturity year.

### issuer (CSV column `issuer_id`)

The entity that issued the instrument and owes the payments. Not necessarily the entity whose credit risk the investor takes: see guarantor.

### guarantor (CSV column `guarantor_id`)

An entity that guarantees the instrument's payments if the issuer does not pay. A guaranteed instrument carries the guarantor's credit risk, so it is rated in line with the guarantor's issuer rating, not the issuer's.

### seniority (CSV column `seniority`)

Where the instrument ranks in repayment if the issuer fails: a concept of the scheme Seniorities.

### currency (CSV column `currency`)

The currency the instrument pays in, as an ISO 4217 code, or one of two local codes: XVD, the Valdorian dollar, the domestic currency of VD; and CVK, the Corvenian krone, the domestic currency of CV. Debt in a currency other than the domestic one of the issuer's country is exposed to that country's transfer and convertibility risk: see Country Ceiling (CC). Debt in the domestic currency is local-currency debt and is not capped by the ceiling.

### maturity (CSV column `maturity`)

The date the instrument is due to be repaid.

### date (CSV column `date`)

The date the action took effect.

### rated thing (CSV column `rated_id`)

What the action is about: an entity (for issuer ratings and Country Ceilings) or an instrument (for instrument ratings).

### rating type (CSV column `rating_type`)

Which kind of rating the action concerns: a concept of the scheme Rating types. Different types are different ratings and are never compared or combined with each other.

### currency basis (CSV column `ccy_basis`)

For issuer ratings, whether the rating covers foreign-currency (FC) or local-currency (LC) obligations: a concept of the scheme Currency bases. An entity can have both, with different values.

### action (CSV column `action`)

What was done: a concept of the scheme Action types. Only upgrades and downgrades change a rating.

### rating (CSV column `rating`)

The rating after the action, on the scale of its rating type. Long-term scale, strongest to weakest: AAA, AA+, AA, AA-, A+, A, A-, BBB+, BBB, BBB-, BB+, BB, BB-, B+, B, B-, CCC, CC, C, RD, D. Investment grade means BBB- or higher; BB+ and below is speculative grade (high yield). RD (restricted default) means the issuer has defaulted on some material obligations, for instance by completing a distressed debt exchange, without being wound up: RD is a default. D means a general default. Short-term scale, strongest to weakest: F1+, F1, F2, F3, B, C, RD, D. WD means the rating was withdrawn (see Withdrawn (WD)): it is not a point on either scale.

### outlook (CSV column `outlook`)

The likely direction of a long-term rating over the next one to two years: a concept of the scheme Outlooks. An outlook is not a rating: revising it does not change the rating.

### rating watch (CSV column `watch`)

Set when the rating is on Rating Watch: a concept of the scheme Rating Watches. A Watch is not a rating change.

### comment (CSV column `comment`)

The analyst's note on the action, such as the reason for a withdrawal.

## Codes

The codes as they appear in the data, grouped by list.

### Rating types

- **LTIDR**, Long-term Issuer Default Rating: The issuer's own creditworthiness: how likely it is to default on its obligations, on the long-term scale. This is what people usually mean by an entity's rating. The foreign-currency (FC) one is the headline rating; there may also be a local-currency (LC) one.
- **STIDR**, Short-term Issuer Default Rating: The issuer's ability to meet obligations falling due within about a year, on the short-term scale (F1+ to D). Not comparable with long-term ratings.
- **INST**, Instrument rating: The rating of one specific instrument. It starts from the issuer's long-term rating and is notched for the instrument's seniority and expected recovery: subordinated debt is usually rated below the issuer, and guaranteed debt is rated on the guarantor. An instrument rating is never the rating of the issuer.
- **CC**, Country Ceiling: Not a rating of anyone. A cap, set for a country, on the foreign-currency ratings of entities domiciled there and of their foreign-currency debt. It reflects transfer and convertibility risk: the risk that the government stops money being converted or sent abroad, so even a sound company could not pay foreign-currency debt. Foreign-currency ratings of entities in the country are generally no higher than the ceiling; local-currency ratings are not capped. A Country Ceiling is not the sovereign's own issuer rating.
- **CO**, Credit opinion: A private, point-in-time view of an entity's credit, given to one client. It is not a published rating and not an issuer rating: never count it as a rating, and never report its value as the entity's rating.

### Currency bases

- **FC**, Foreign currency: Covers obligations in foreign currency, so it is subject to the Country Ceiling.
- **LC**, Local currency: Covers obligations in the currency of the entity's own country; not subject to the Country Ceiling.

### Action types

- **AS**, Assigned: A first rating is given.
- **AF**, Affirmed: The rating is reviewed and kept the same. The outlook may be revised in the same action; that does not make it an upgrade or a downgrade.
- **UG**, Upgraded: The rating is raised.
- **DG**, Downgraded: The rating is lowered. The only action that counts as a downgrade.
- **RW**, Placed on Rating Watch: The rating is flagged as likely to change soon, usually within about six months, in the direction rating watch gives. The rating itself is unchanged: not a downgrade.
- **WD**, Withdrawn: The agency stops maintaining the rating, often for commercial reasons (the issuer no longer pays for it, or the debt is repaid). After a withdrawal the entity has no rating of that type. A withdrawal is not a credit event: it says nothing about default, and is not a downgrade.
- **EX**, Expected rating: A rating signalled before an instrument's final documents are signed. It is not yet a rating: the instrument stays unrated until an Assigned (AS) action confirms it.

### Outlooks

- **STA**, Stable: No direction expected.
- **POS**, Positive: The rating may go up over one to two years.
- **NEG**, Negative: The rating may go down over one to two years. Not a downgrade.

### Rating Watches

- **RWN**, Rating Watch Negative: A downgrade is likely in the near term. Not a downgrade yet.

### Seniorities

- **SU**, Senior unsecured: Ranks with the issuer's ordinary debts; usually rated at the level of the issuer's long-term rating.
- **SUB**, Subordinated: Repaid only after senior debt, so investors recover less in a default; usually rated one or more notches below the issuer's long-term rating.

### Entity types

- **CORP**, Corporate: A non-financial company.
- **BANK**, Bank: A bank.
- **FINSUB**, Finance subsidiary: A subsidiary that exists to raise debt for its parent group. It usually has no issuer rating of its own; its debt is guaranteed by the parent, so investors take the parent's credit risk.
- **SOV**, Sovereign: A national government. A sovereign record can carry a Country Ceiling, issuer ratings, or both: they are different things, and the ceiling is not the sovereign's rating.

## The data

These tables are the data after its rules have been applied: duplicate records merged into one entity (record_ids lists the records each entity merges), and each rating with the period it held. An empty valid_to means the rating was still in force on 1 October 2026. ancestors lists every entity above each entity in its chain of parents, with how many levels up.

Q6. Why is Valdoria Telecom's foreign-currency rating lower than its local-currency rating?

  • R1 csv run 1: Names the BB Country Ceiling as the cause, but never says the ceiling caps only foreign-currency ratings and not local-currency ones; it only lists the differing ratings.
  • R1 csv run 4: Identifies the BB country ceiling and FC rating at that level, but hedges that the data states no reason and doesn't explicitly say the ceiling caps FC but not LC ratings.
  • R1 csv run 5: Identifies the BB Country Ceiling matching the foreign-currency rating, but hedges that the data doesn't state the cause and never says the ceiling applies only to foreign-currency ratings, not local-currency ones.
  • R2 rdf run 1: Identifies the BB country ceiling equal to the FC rating as consistent with a cap, but hedges it as inference and never states that the ceiling caps FC and not LC ratings.
  • R2 rdf run 2: Links the FC rating of BB to the Valdoria country ceiling (BB) but hedges that the data doesn't state a reason, and never says the ceiling caps foreign-currency but not local-currency ratings.
  • R2 rdf run 3: Mentions the BB Country Ceiling as consistent with the gap but declines to name it as the cause and never says it caps only foreign-currency ratings.
  • R2 rdf run 4: Mentions the BB Country Ceiling as consistent with the gap but hedges that no reason is stated, and never says the ceiling caps foreign-currency but not local-currency ratings.
  • R2 rdf run 5: Links BB foreign-currency rating to the VD country ceiling but only as a hedged inference; doesn't assert the ceiling as the cause or state that it caps foreign-currency but not local-currency ratings.
  • R3 csv+padding run 1: Names the BB country ceiling as the cause, but doesn't say it caps only foreign-currency ratings and not local-currency ones (only implied by the ratings listed).
  • R3 csv+padding run 2: Identifies the BB Country Ceiling and that the foreign-currency rating sits at it, but hedges and does not clearly state that the ceiling caps foreign-currency but not local-currency ratings as the cause.
  • R3 csv+padding run 3: Names the BB country ceiling and the FC/LC rating gap, but hedges that no reason is stated and never says the ceiling caps foreign-currency ratings and not local-currency ones.
  • R3 csv+padding run 4: Names the BB country ceiling and notes it equals the FC rating, but hedges ('no stated reason') and never says the ceiling caps FC ratings and not LC ratings.
  • R3 csv+padding run 5: Identifies the BB Valdoria ceiling and FC BB vs LC BBB- as consistent, but hedges that the data states no reason and never says the ceiling caps FC ratings but not LC ones.
  • R10 executed run 2: Names the BB Country Ceiling as the likely cause, but hedges and never states that the ceiling caps foreign-currency ratings and not local-currency ones.
  • R10 executed run 3: Identifies the BB ceiling and that FC sits at it while LC is higher, but hedges that the data doesn't state the cause and only says it is 'consistent with' the ceiling capping FC only, rather than asserting it.
  • R10 executed run 4: Identifies the BB Country Ceiling and that it is consistent with capping FC, but hedges as unconfirmed and doesn't clearly state it caps FC and not LC ratings as the cause.
  • R7 csv+neutral run 5: Names the BB Country Ceiling as the cause, but never says the ceiling applies only to foreign-currency ratings and not local-currency ones (LC is only shown as higher at BBB-).
  • R8 csv+axioms run 1: Mentions the Country Ceiling of BB alongside FC BB, but says the data gives no reason and never states the ceiling caps foreign-currency ratings and not local-currency ones.
  • R8 csv+axioms run 2: Names the BB Country Ceiling as consistent with the FC rating, but hedges that no reason is stated and never says the ceiling caps FC and not LC ratings.
  • R8 csv+axioms run 3: Identifies the BB Valdoria ceiling and the higher local-currency rating, but hedges that the data states no reason and never says the ceiling caps foreign-currency but not local-currency ratings.
  • R8 csv+axioms run 5: Notes FC rating equals the BB Country Ceiling but disclaims a stated cause and never says the ceiling caps FC and not LC ratings.
  • R9 csv+axioms+markdown run 1: Identifies the BB Country Ceiling matching the FC rating and says LC is not capped, but never states that the ceiling is the cause or that it caps foreign-currency ratings specifically.
